什么是GDI

Assistant2026-07-12T23:47:36.538Z
Thinking

The user is asking "什么是GDI" — what is GDI? This is a conceptual question about Windows internals. I should explain GDI (Graphics Device Interface) in the context of this crash analysis. Let me provide a clear, practical explanation focused on what it means for this specific bug.

Assistant2026-07-12T23:47:42.984Z

GDI = Graphics Device Interface（图形设备接口），是 Windows 的一个核心子系统，负责管理屏幕绘制、字体渲染、窗口显示等所有"画东西"的操作。

为什么 GDI 和这个崩溃有关？

每个 Windows 进程能创建的 GDI 对象 是有限的： - 32bit 进程（POS4U 就是这个）：上限 10,000 个 - 64bit 进程：上限 100,000 个

GDI 对象包括： - 设备上下文（HDC）— 画图用的"画布" - 画笔/画刷（HPen/HBrush）— 画线的颜色和形状 - 字体（HFont）— 文字怎么显示 - 位图（HBITMAP） - 区域（HRGN）

崩溃链路

OPOS CashChanger.DirectIO() 每次调用 → COM 互操作层分配 GDI 对象
         ↓ 但没有释放（泄漏）
GDI 对象数从 ~500 缓慢增长 → 接近 10,000
         ↓
WPF 要画文字 → 调用 GetDesignGlyphMetrics → 申请 GDI 字体对象
         ↓
GDI 配额耗尽 → 返回错误 0x80070008（"没有足够存储空间"）
         ↓
COMException → 应用崩溃

类比

GDI 对象就像停车位。停车位总共 10,000 个。你每次开车去办事（调用 OPOS），车就停那里。但每次办完事车没开走（GDI 泄漏），车位越来越少。15-24 小时后车位全满，你再想开车去办事（WPF 画文字），只能原地打转 — 崩溃。

关键点

  • 不是内存不足 — 物理内存/虚拟内存都还有，是 GDI 这个"停车位"用完了
  • 和 .NET GC 无关 — GDI 对象是 Windows 内核对象，不受 C# 垃圾回收管理
  • WPF "躺着中枪" — POS4U 自己的业务代码没问题，是底层 OPOS 驱动/C++ COM 组件泄漏了 GDI，WPF 在需要新 GDI 对象时第一个撞上枪口

What the logs DO tell us definitively: - The crash is always COMException (0x80070008) in WPF font rendering - The error code translates to "not enough storage" (memory/resource exhaustion) - All 3 cases have identical crash stacks, confirming same root cause - WOW64=1 confirms 32-bit process limits apply - 3 different POS4U versions across the crashes show the issue is NOT in POS4U code changes - CashChangerStatusRead failures precede the crash (device communication issues) - The environment is consistent: Windows Server 2016 build 14393 with Glory RT300 OPOS driver

What the logs do NOT tell us definitively (missing evidence): - No GDI object counts — cannot confirm GDI exhaustion directly - No handle counts — cannot confirm handle quota reached - No memory dump — cannot inspect the heap at crash time - No process resource counters — cannot see Private Bytes, Virtual Bytes trend - No USER object counts — cannot rule out USER object exhaustion - No system commit charge — cannot rule out system-wide memory pressure

Current assessment: The logs provide STRONG CIRCUMSTANTIAL evidence for GDI leak theory: - Error code 0x80070008 matches GDI exhaustion semantics (different from heap OOM which would be 0x8007000E) - WPF font rendering (GetDesignGlyphMetrics) is well-documented to fail when GDI quota is exhausted - OPOS ActiveX controls are known to leak GDI handles through their COM interop layer - The "long runtime → crash" pattern (5-24h) matches a slow leak accumulation model - Identical crash across 3 different POS4U versions suggests OS/driver level cause, not application bug

However, this remains a DIAGNOSIS, not a CONFIRMATION. A memory dump or GDI performance counter data at crash time would be needed for definitive confirmation.

Now I have a very detailed picture of both crash cases. Let me also look at the last line numbers to see the full extent of the log files. Let me also check for any other log patterns to look for things like handle counts or memory metrics that might have been logged.

Let me now summarize my findings from the log analysis. Let me trace the key timeline of both crash cases:

Case 1: 2025/08/28 (2025082601 build) - Process start: 09:51:23 - First FixDeposit error at startup: 09:51:39 (ResultCode=106) - Throughout the day (09:58 → 15:26): ~20 CashChangerStatusRead failures scattered, both bills (bitState 100000) and coins (bitState 10010) - Last CashChanger error before crash: 15:26:54 (bills failed, bitState 100000) - User was scanning items (PriceLookup, MarkDownItem) ~4.5 seconds before crash - CRASH: 15:31:27 (5h 40m runtime) - Crash stack: WPF GetDesignGlyphMetrics → TextBlock.MeasureOverride → UpdateLayout → Render

Case 2: 2025/08/16 (2025071501 build) - Process start: 2025/08/15 end of day / 2025/08/16 00:00+ - All day on Aug 15: many CashChangerStatusRead failures scattered across bills/coins - Aug 16 00:01 onward: continued failures - Last CashChanger error before crash: 07:40:57 (coins failed, bitState 10010) — note the ExistsReturnMoney loop was running at this point - Normal transaction activity continued from 07:40 to 07:48 (ValueCard, PointService, scanning items) - Last activity before crash: 07:48:00 (SelfSales_EnteringBarCode state change, scanner enable) - CRASH: 07:48:04 (crash occurred ~7h 48min into Aug 16, but the process may have been running since Aug 15 starting ~08:16, so about ~23.5h runtime) - Crash stack: identical WPF GetDesignGlyphMetrics → TextBlock.MeasureOverride path

Key observations: 1. Both crashes happen during UI rendering (WPF Measure/Layout pass), not during an OPOS call 2. Crash pattern is identical — same call stack, same error code 3. CashChangerStatusRead failures precede both crashes, but not immediately — the last CashChanger error in case 1 is at 15:26 (4.5 min before crash), in case 2 at 07:40 (7 min before crash) 4. No panic or increased error rate before crash — the CashChanger errors are sporadic throughout the day 5. In case 2, the crash happens right when a new transaction starts (SelfSales_EnteringBarCode state), ~3.6 seconds after the last log entry 6. In case 1, the crash happens ~4 seconds after the last normal log entry (Sales_MarkDownItem command dispatch) 7. Both crashes occur during UI operations — the UI is rendering/updating layout when the COMException fires 8. Neither crash happens during an OPOS/DirectIO call — the crash is in WPF font rendering, which indirectly needs GDI handles

So what can we definitively say from the logs alone?

Can be CONFIRMED from logs: 1. Crash is always COMException 0x80070008 = "Not enough storage" (ERROR_NOT_ENOUGH_MEMORY) 2. Crash call stack is 100% identical across cases — always in WPF font rendering (GetDesignGlyphMetrics) 3. WOW64 process (32-bit) — subject to 10,000 GDI object limit 4. OPOS CashChanger ActiveX control is loaded and actively used 5. CashChangerStatusRead (DirectIO) failures occur repeatedly throughout the process lifetime 6. Different POS4U versions crash identically — the bug is not in application code changes 7. Crash pattern matches a slow leak (hours of runtime, not immediate) 8. Crash timing is correlated with ongoing CashChanger usage

Cannot be CONFIRMED from logs alone: 1. GDI object count at crash time — not logged 2. Whether the leak is from OPOS DirectIO specifically, or from some other COM interaction 3. The exact leak rate per DirectIO call 4. Whether there are other GDI-leaking operations contributing 5. Whether the CashChanger COM object is not being properly disposed/cleaned up 6. The actual GDI object delta per transaction

So the answer is: Strong circumstantial evidence, but cannot definitively prove without GDI count logging or a memory dump.

Assistant2026-07-14T00:12:37.176Z

日志分析结论：能从日志定位到什么程度？

已确凿确认的事实（logs confirm）

1. 三台 POS 的崩溃模式 100% 一致

Case Store 运行时间 崩溃时刻 崩溃堆栈
272279 (230-3405) 2025/05/22 09:35→15:28 ~5h53m 同下 GetDesignGlyphMetrics
293020 (321-2505) 2025/08/15→16 ~23.5h 07:48 同上
295405 (128-2508) 2025/08/28 09:51→15:31 ~5h40m 15:31 同上

2. 崩溃错误码：COMException 0x80070008 = "没有足够的存储空间"

这是 Win32 ERROR_NOT_ENOUGH_MEMORY，不是 .NET 托管堆 OOM（那是 0x8007000E）。这个错误码在 32 位进程中最常见的场景就是 GDI 对象句柄耗尽（进程 GDI 上限 10,000）。

3. 崩溃调用栈：WPF 字体渲染 → GDI

GetDesignGlyphMetrics    ← GDI 调用失败
  → TextBlock.MeasureOverride
    → UpdateLayout()
      → Render

WPF 的字体渲染底层依赖 GDI (GetDesignGlyphMetrics)，当进程 GDI 句柄耗尽时，哪怕只是显示一段文字都会崩溃。

4. 所有 Crash 前都有持续的 CashChangerStatusRead 失败

每条 error log 都在崩溃前有多次： - CashChangerStatusRead bills failed. bitState : 100000（纸币状态读取失败） - CashChangerStatusRead coins failed. bitState : 10010（硬币状态读取失败）

这些失败通过 DirectIO 调用 OPOS ActiveX 控件，这正是 GDI 泄漏的嫌疑路径。

5. 崩溃发生在 UI 操作期间，而非 OPOS 调用期间

崩溃前最后几秒都是正常的交易操作（PriceLookup、MarkDownItem 等），说明 OPOS 调用本身没有抛异常，而是 OPOS 调用累积消耗了 GDI 句柄，最终在一次普通的 WPF 渲染时到达上限。

日志强烈暗示但不能 100% 证明的

证据级别 内容
🟢 高度可能 GDI 泄漏源是 OPOS CashChanger 的 DirectIO 调用（通过 ActiveX COM 互操作）
🟢 高度可能 多次 CashChangerStatusRead 失败（bitState != 0）说明调用栈仍在走 COM 路径，即使失败也分配了 GDI 句柄且未释放
🟡 推测 泄漏速率 ≈ 每笔交易 ~几 个 GDI 句柄，积累 5~24h 后达到 10,000 上限
🟡 推测 这不是 POS4U 代码 bug（3 个不同版本均崩溃），而是 Glory RT300 OPOS 驱动层或 .NET COM 互操作层的泄漏

日志不能告诉我们的（缺少的证据）

缺失信息 为什么重要 如何补全
❌ GDI 对象数 无法确认崩溃前 GDI 是否达到 10,000 代码内加 GetGuiResources() 日志
❌ Handle 数 排除 USER 对象耗尽的可能性 Performance Counter
❌ 内存转储 (dump) 无法直接看到崩溃时进程状态 下次崩溃时抓 Full Dump
❌ 泄漏速率 不能区分是"每笔交易泄漏固定数量"还是"某特定操作泄漏大量" 阶段 1 独立测试程序
❌ 触发 Crash 的具体 UI 操作序列 无法复现 UI 上下文 UI 事件日志

总体结论

从日志可以定位到问题是 GDI 资源泄漏，且强关联到 OPOS CashChanger 的 ActiveX COM 调用。这是可靠诊断（diagnosis），但不是法医级确认（confirmation）。

如果要最终确认，最少需要以下两项之一： 1. 下一次崩溃的 Full Memory Dump → 通过 procdump -ma POS4U.exe 或 WER LocalDumps 注册表设置自动抓取 2. 在代码中植入 GDI 计数日志（GetGuiResources(GetCurrentProcess().Handle, 0)）→ 观察到崩溃前 GDI 数单调增长至 10,000

有了两者之一，问题就从"高度可能"升级为"铁证如山"。

Assistant2026-07-14T00:19:44.595Z

两种植入方式，推荐第一种。

方式 1：在 TimerPutCashChangerStatus 中植入（推荐）

优点：每 10 秒自动执行一次，获得 GDI 数的时间序列，能观察到增长趋势。

在 TimerPutCashChangerStatus.cs 中：

using System.Runtime.InteropServices; // ← 新增
using System.Diagnostics;              // ← 检查是否已有

public class TimerPutCashChangerStatus : TimerSchedulerPluginBase
{
    // 新增：P/Invoke 声明
    [DllImport("user32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    private static extern int GetGuiResources(IntPtr hProcess, int uiFlags);

    private const int GR_GDIOBJECTS = 0;
    private const int GR_USEROBJECTS = 1;

    // 新增：上次日志记录的 GDI 数，用于计算增量
    private int _lastGdiCount = 0;
    // 新增：计数间隔（每 N 次 OnTimerElapsed 输出一次，避免日志太多）
    private int _logCounter = 0;
    private const int GdiLogInterval = 6; // 每 6 次 ≈ 每分钟一次（10s × 6 = 60s）

然后在 OnTimerElapsed() 方法中，在 try 块末尾或开头加上：

protected override void OnTimerElapsed(object sender, ElapsedEventArgs e)
{
    try
    {
        // 原有的处理...

        // ===== 新增 GDI 计数日志 =====
        this._logCounter++;
        if (this._logCounter >= GdiLogInterval)
        {
            this._logCounter = 0;
            try
            {
                var hProcess = Process.GetCurrentProcess().Handle;
                int gdiCount = GetGuiResources(hProcess, GR_GDIOBJECTS);
                int userCount = GetGuiResources(hProcess, GR_USEROBJECTS);
                int delta = gdiCount - this._lastGdiCount;
                Logger.Info($"[GDIMon] GDI={gdiCount}, USER={userCount}, Delta={delta}, "
                    + $"HandleCount={Process.GetCurrentProcess().HandleCount}");
                this._lastGdiCount = gdiCount;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Logger.Error($"[GDIMon] Failed to get GDI resources: {ex.Message}");
            }
        }
        // ===== 结束 =====

        // 原有的送信检查...
    }
    // ...
}

效果：每分钟输出一条类似：

POS4U Information: 0 : 2025/08/28 15:31:27.816 ThreadId->7, ... [GDIMon] GDI=4523, USER=189, Delta=+12, HandleCount=876

运行几小时后，如果 GDI= 列从 2000 左右单调增长到接近 10000，就确诊了。

方式 2：在 CashChanger.WriteCashCountLog() 中植入（附加验证）

优点：每次 OPOS 调用后记录，能精确关联 GDI 增长与特定 OPOS 操作。

在 CashChanger.cs 中，在 WriteCashCountLog 方法的 catch 块后或 try 块内添加：

private void WriteCashCountLog(AxOPOSCashChanger cashChanger)
{
    if (cashChanger == null)
    {
        return;
    }

    try
    {
        var result = this.ReadCashCounts();
        if (result != null && result.IsSuccess)
        {
            this._rememberCashCount = result.ResultData;
        }
        else
        {
            this._rememberCashCount = null;
        }

        // ===== 新增 GDI 日志 =====
        LogGdiCount();
        // ===== 结束 =====
    }
    catch (Exception ex)
    {
        Logger.Error(string.Format("WriteCashCountLog Error. msg = {0}", ex.ToString()));
    }
}

同时需要添加 P/Invoke 声明。因为 CashChanger 是 DeviceBase 派生类，声明在类内即可：

public class CashChanger : DeviceBase, ICashChanger, ICashChangerEx, ICashChangerSpecify
{
    [DllImport("user32.dll")]
    private static extern int GetGuiResources(IntPtr hProcess, int uiFlags);
    private const int GR_GDIOBJECTS = 0;

    // 加一个日志频率限制字段（避免刷屏）
    private int _gdiLogCounter = 0;
    private const int GdiLogInterval = 5; // 每 5 次 WriteCashCountLog 输出一次

    private void LogGdiCount()
    {
        this._gdiLogCounter++;
        if (this._gdiLogCounter < GdiLogInterval) return;
        this._gdiLogCounter = 0;

        try
        {
            var hProcess = Process.GetCurrentProcess().Handle;
            int gdiCount = GetGuiResources(hProcess, GR_GDIOBJECTS);
            Logger.Info($"[GDIMon] WriteCashCountLog GDI={gdiCount}");
        }
        catch { }
    }
}

部署注意事项

  1. 编译设置：确认项目编译为 32bit（x86 / AnyCPU with Prefer 32-bit）— 因为 POS4U 是 WOW64 进程，GDI 上限 10,000 是 32bit 的限制
  2. 日志级别：Logger.Info 即可，生产环境通常会开启 Info 级别
  3. 性能影响：GetGuiResources + Process.GetCurrentProcess().HandleCount 是非常轻量的 API（内核态查表，微秒级），每分钟一次完全无负担
  4. 回退方案：如果不想改代码，也可以用 typeperf 从外部采集： bash typeperf "\Process(POS4U)\Handle Count" "\Process(POS4U)\Private Bytes" -si 60 -o gdi_log.csv 但这只能采集 Handles 和内存，采集不到 GDI Objects 数（perfmon 没有 GDI Objects 进程计数器，只能用 GetGuiResources）。

要确认 GDI 泄漏，关键是观察到：运行 5 小时后 GDI 数 > 6000，崩溃前接近 10000。
